## `AGO-4771-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** 3D-Body-Scanner Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Haute Couture 3D Body Laser Scanning & Bespoke Fitter
